- Resolve packages (pubspec.lock, plugin registrants): `(cd packages/danaview_editor_fonts && flutter pub get)`
- Resolve packages (pubspec.lock, plugin registrants): `(cd packages/danaview_engine && flutter pub get)`
- Resolve packages (pubspec.lock, plugin registrants): `(cd packages/danaview_features && flutter pub get)`
- Resolve packages (pubspec.lock, plugin registrants): `(cd packages/danaview_platform && flutter pub get)`
- Resolve packages (pubspec.lock, plugin registrants): `(cd packages/danaview_transcription && flutter pub get)`
- Resolve packages (pubspec.lock, plugin registrants): `(cd packages/danaview_ui_kit && flutter pub get)`
- Resolve packages (pubspec.lock, plugin registrants): `(cd packages/danaview_whisper && flutter pub get)`
- Pigeon output: `(cd packages/danaview_editor_engine && dart run pigeon --input pigeons/engine_api.dart)`
- ffigen bindings (needs libclang) (off macOS without libclang: provisional rule-mapped file): `(cd packages/danaview_whisper && dart run ffigen --config ffigen.yaml)`
- Codec golden bodies and kitchen_sink.dnproj: `(cd packages/danaview_editor_core && DANAVIEW_UPDATE_GOLDENS=1 dart test test/codec/determinism_test.dart)`
- Schema fixtures (.dnproj headers and checksums): `(cd packages/danaview_editor_core && REGENERATE_FIXTURES=1 dart test test/codec_migrations/fixtures_test.dart)`
- .vsprite fixtures and sprites.json **(macOS only)**: `(cd packages/danaview_editor_engine_api && DANAVIEW_REGEN_SPRITE_FIXTURES=1 flutter test test/render_prep/sprite_fixtures_test.dart)`
- Reference vectors and goldens (expect no change): `(cd packages/danaview_editor_engine_api && dart run tool/regen_goldens.dart)`
- CocoaPods (Podfile.lock) **(macOS only)**: `(cd ios && pod install)`
- CocoaPods (Podfile.lock) **(macOS only)**: `(cd macos && pod install)`
- CocoaPods (Podfile.lock) **(macOS only)**: `(cd packages/danaview_editor_engine/example/ios && pod install)`
- RECOMPILE the spike metallibs and BUILD_INFO.txt **(macOS only)**: `(cd spikes/ios_engine && bash tools/build_metallibs.sh)`

## Local folders

Rename the local folders at cutover, and move Claude's notes folder to match: Mac `/Users/besaoct/Desktop/vwish_player` -> `danaview_player` (notes: `~/.claude/projects/-Users-besaoct-Desktop-vwish-player` -> `-Users-besaoct-Desktop-danaview-player`); Windows `…\Desktop\vwish` -> `…\Desktop\danaview_player` (notes: `%USERPROFILE%\.claude\projects\C--Users-Masud-OneDrive---Zota-Healthcare-Ltd-Desktop-vwish` -> `…-Desktop-danaview-player`).

## Mac checklist (cannot be verified on Windows)

- Rename the checkout folder (…/vwish_player -> …/danaview_player) before anything else, and move Claude's notes folder to match (~/.claude/projects/-Users-besaoct-Desktop-vwish-player -> …-danaview-player).
- --regenerate on the Mac: pod install (ios, macos, editor engine example), the .vsprite fixtures (only text_box_stroke_shadow may change), ffigen for real (no diff expected), RECOMPILE the 4 spike metallibs; then `--verify` with no flags must pass.
- Metal: the renamed kernels (dv_look, dv_blur_h/v, dv_sharpen, dv_look_st, dv_blur_h_st, ...) and their Swift lookups in DVSpikeKernels/DVSpikeStitchable; `strings *.metallib | grep -i -e vw -e /Users` must be empty.
- Podspecs: danaview_whisper, danaview_editor_engine (resource bundle DVEditorKernels), the spike pods DVSpikeKernels and DVSpikeStitchable; `pod lib lint` or a clean `pod install`.
- Swift: the editor engine plugin (Pigeon output, DVPK waveforms, DVTH thumbnails, DVEditorKernels loader), danaview_whisper (dv_whisper_forwarder.cpp, DynamicLibrary.open('danaview_whisper.framework/danaview_whisper'), os_log subsystem com.vecvel.danaview.whisper).
- Xcode projects: PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER com.vecvel.danaview and com.vecvel.danaview.RunnerTests (ios, macos, example), danaview.app / TEST_HOST, schemes, ExportOptions.plist, entitlements, CFBundleURLSchemes danaview, CFBundleURLName.
- Builds and tests: flutter build ios --debug --no-codesign, flutter build macos --debug, the example app's RunnerTests (XCTest), the whisper host tests (src/tests, dv_test.h) with DV_SANITIZER.
- The spike host app (DVSpikeHost, DVSpikeTests, DVSpikeUITests): builds, Info.plist name "DV Spike".
- fastlane: deliver/supply dry runs with the new Appfiles and metadata; new App Store and Play records for com.vecvel.danaview.
